**THE OVERSHOOT GROWS.** *A relaxation loosening in $n$ does not tighten to $\sqrt{3/2}$ at $n=12$*, so the branch in which T9 proves Conjecture A there is closed on the trend rather than on one measurement, and [post F42](fence-posts.md)'s original conclusion is what the data supports. **This is the measurement [r331](../reviews/REVIEW_r331.md) and [r332](../reviews/REVIEW_r332.md) correctly said had never been made**, and their reading of the post was right in its own terms: the earlier `1.5`--`3.7x` figure was a single-row LP and could not support the conclusion drawn from it. *The conclusion happens to survive; the evidence for it did not, and now does.*

**What is NOT settled by this.** The $n=6$ figure was already on the record and flagged as doubtful for sitting outside the conjecture's range; the $n=8$ figure is new, and **the trend across the two carries the conclusion rather than either point**. Two points are two points. *Reported as a run, not as a proof: an LP maximum over a relaxation bounds nothing below, so this closes a route and establishes no inequality.*

#### From [I77](../plan.md): the DUAL bound, closed-form for every spectrum, and exact at the balanced product

**Working notes, Gate 2 pending, derived 2026-09-10.** *The programme's vertex enumeration is unnecessary, and LP duality turns it into a minimax.* **THE ONE-DIMENSIONAL READING IS WITHDRAWN AT THE POINT IT IS ASSERTED**: the uniform $y,z$ family below is a strict SUB-family of the LP dual, off by twenty times at padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$'s spectrum at $n=12$, where it returns $221/\sqrt6=90.2229$ against an LP optimum of $11/\sqrt6=4.4907$ ([r871](../reviews/REVIEW_r871.md), the retraction having stood only further down until [r876](../reviews/REVIEW_r876.md) found it here). **Suppose real $y,z$ satisfy $$\lambda_\alpha\lambda_\beta\lambda_\gamma\ \le\ y+\tfrac z3(\lambda_\alpha+\lambda_\beta+\lambda_\gamma)\qquad\text{at every triple of distinct indices.}$$ Multiplying by $T_{\alpha\beta\gamma}\ge0$ and summing gives $m_3\le2(n-2)Ny$**, the $z$ term vanishing because symmetry turns it into $z\sum_\alpha\lambda_\alpha\sum_{\beta\gamma}T_{\alpha\beta\gamma}=2(n-2)z\operatorname{tr}W=0$. *That step needs the marginal to be CONSTANT in the index, not merely trace-freeness* ([r872](../reviews/REVIEW_r872.md)). *So $$\theta\ \le\ \sum_\alpha\lambda_\alpha^3+(n-2)N\cdot\min_z\ \max_{\alpha\ne\beta\ne\gamma}\Big[\lambda_\alpha\lambda_\beta\lambda_\gamma-\tfrac z3(\lambda_\alpha+\lambda_\beta+\lambda_\gamma)\Big],$$* **using only [Lemma L153](bounds.md)'s marginal, the nonnegativity of $T$, and trace-freeness.** *No caps, no decomposability, no criticality, and no relation (B).*

> **IT IS EXACT AT THE BALANCED PRODUCT, CHECKED AT TWO DIMENSIONS.** *At $S^p\times S^p$ the normalised spectrum is $\lambda_+=1/\sqrt{(p-1)(2p-1)}$ with multiplicity $p(p-1)$ and $\lambda_-=-\tfrac1p\sqrt{(p-1)/(2p-1)}$ with multiplicity $p^2$, verified against $\lVert W\rVert=1$.* **At $n=4$, $p=2$**: the witness is $z=1/4$ and $y=\sqrt3/36$, and the bound is $\tfrac1{2\sqrt3}+\tfrac1{\sqrt3}=\sqrt3/2=\theta_0(4)$ EXACTLY, the earlier $0.8659$ against $0.8660254$ having been a rounding artefact ([r873](../reviews/REVIEW_r873.md)). **At $n=12$, $p=6$**: $z=1/220$ EXACTLY and the bound is $\sqrt{55}/6=\theta_0(12)=1.2360331$ exactly, the earlier $1.2362$ and $0.004544$ having been rounding artefacts ([r873](../reviews/REVIEW_r873.md)). *Both agree, and the symbolic identity below shows the agreement is exact at every even $n$.* **The $n=4$ witness is machine-checked in exact arithmetic and carries no gap axiom** in [L203DualBound.lean](../lean/HamiltonsCubic/Results/L203DualBound.lean): the spectrum's unit norm and trace-freeness, the bound evaluating to $\sqrt3/2$, and the all-within and all-cross triples both ACTIVE at $z=1/4$.

> **AND THE AGREEMENT IS AN EXACT IDENTITY AT EVERY EVEN $n$, NOT A NUMERICAL COINCIDENCE.** *Derived symbolically 2026-09-10.* **Write $D=\sqrt{(p-1)(2p-1)}$, so the balanced product's normalised spectrum is $P=1/D$ with multiplicity $p(p-1)$ and $-Q$ with $Q=(p-1)/(pD)$ and multiplicity $p^2$**, which is trace-free and unit by $(p-1)(2p-1)=D^2$, *and $\sum_\alpha\lambda_\alpha^3=1/(pD)$.* **The active pair is the all-within and the one-within-two-cross triple**, not the all-within and all-cross: balancing them gives $$z=\tfrac32P(P-Q),\qquad y=\tfrac12P^2(3Q-P)=\frac{2p-3}{2pD^3},$$ *and the remaining two functions sit strictly below at that $z$.* **Then $(n-2)N=2p(p-1)(2p-1)$ turns the second term into $(2p-3)/D$ exactly, and** $$\theta\ \le\ \frac1{pD}+\frac{2p-3}D=\frac{(2p-1)(p-1)}{pD}=\frac Dp=\frac{\sqrt{(p-1)(2p-1)}}p=\theta_0(2p).$$ *The last step is $\theta_0(2p)=\sqrt{2(2p-1)(2p-2)}/(2p)$ simplified, so the two agree identically in $p$ rather than at the two dimensions checked by hand.* **Both halves are machine-checked and gap-free** in [L203DualBound.lean](../lean/HamiltonsCubic/Results/L203DualBound.lean): the collapse of $1/(pD)+(2p-3)/D$ to $D/p$, and $\theta_0(2p)=D/p$. *Every declaration there rests on the three standard axioms alone, the file is in the root import, and every literal matches this block* ([r873](../reviews/REVIEW_r873.md)).

> **AND THE $n=4$ CASE IS A DEGENERATE INSTANCE, WHICH THE FIRST PASS READ AS TIGHTNESS.** *At $p=2$ there are only two within-factor planes, so a triple of DISTINCT within indices does not exist and the all-within class is EMPTY: the constraint at it is vacuous and imposing it can only weaken the bound.* **It happens to sit at the same value there**, which is why dropping it changes neither $z=1/4$ nor $y=1/(12\sqrt3)$, *so the bound and its exactness survive while the claim that three functions are simultaneously active does not.* **The genuinely active pair at $p=2$ is the one-within-two-cross and all-cross triples**, and from $p=3$ it is the all-within and one-within-two-cross pair the general computation names. **The machine-checked file is correct about the arithmetic, and its docstrings carried the retracted reading in three places until [r873](../reviews/REVIEW_r873.md) found them.** *The class that binds at $p=2$ alongside the all-cross one is the one-within-two-cross, whose dual function is constant there because $\lambda_+-2\lvert\lambda_-\rvert=0$, and it now has its own theorem. Dropping the empty class also costs $z=1/4$ its uniqueness: the minimax is attained on all of $[-\tfrac12,\tfrac14]$.*

> **AND THE REDUCTION BUILT ON IT IS FALSE, WHICH THE ROUND SETTLED BY EXHIBITING THE SPECTRUM.** *[r872](../reviews/REVIEW_r872.md).* **The bound is VALID, and its maximum over spectra at $n=12$ is not $\theta_0(12)$**: the coordinate-diagonal Ricci-flat Weyl tensor of the six-block partition has dual bound $3\sqrt{165}/10=3.853570$, *above $\theta_0(12)=1.236033$ and above the record's own standing $3.0509$, with a matching primal $T$.* **So the sentence that the conjecture follows from maximising this bound is withdrawn**, and the programme's reach is the same shape its two-eigenvalue pass had: exact at the balanced product, and weaker than the record elsewhere. *The crossover reading goes with it: nothing here crosses over, because the maximum is above the competitor's value at $n=12$ already.*

> **WHAT SURVIVES IS A TIGHTNESS THEOREM AT THE MAXIMISER, AND IT IS GENUINE RATHER THAN A COINCIDENCE.** *Complementary slackness holds exactly at the balanced product* ([r872](../reviews/REVIEW_r872.md)), **so the balanced product's own $T$ IS optimal for the programme and the agreement is not an artefact of the dual choice.** *With the identity above, that says the relaxation's value at the balanced product is exactly $\theta_0(n)$ at every even $n$, which is a structural statement about why that tensor is extremal for this relaxation.* **And the bound needs even less than Ricci-flatness**, holding for every unit-norm symmetric $W$ whose eigenbasis satisfies the marginal ([r872](../reviews/REVIEW_r872.md)). *What it is not is a route to the conjecture.*
> **WHY BOTH RELAXATIONS FAILED THE SAME WAY, WHICH IS A STATEMENT ABOUT $T$ AND NOT ABOUT EITHER ARGUMENT.** *Working notes, Gate 2 pending.* **THE RELAXATION WITH L153'S MARGINALS AS ITS ONLY INPUT ABOUT $T$ IS CLOSED, WITH A NUMBER: its EXACT LP optimum is $11/\sqrt6=4.4907$ at $n=12$** ([r871](../reviews/REVIEW_r871.md)), *above the record's standing $3.0509$, so no choice of DUAL multipliers saves that constraint set, which is what the two failed repair attempts below were reaching for. **Relation (B) is NOT in that constraint set and adding it is the repair the reviewer recommended**, since it is linear in $T$ at fixed spectrum and admissible in the very same programme with $N$ extra multipliers. *The primal is a maximisation, so adding (B) SHRINKS the feasible set and the augmented optimum is at most $4.4907$: the figure bounds the repair from above rather than ruling it out, and the earlier reading had the inequality backwards* ([r876](../reviews/REVIEW_r876.md)). **The screen that settles it: the optimal primal $T_{123}=10$ gives $-20c$ at index $1$ where (B) demands $-4c$, so that optimiser is infeasible for (B) and the augmented optimum is STRICTLY smaller.** *And the rest of the paragraph was attempts at something that cannot work.* **And the uniform family is a strict sub-family of the dual**: at padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$'s spectrum at $n=12$ it returns $221/\sqrt6=90.2229$ where the LP optimum is $4.4907$, so "duality turns it into a one-dimensional minimax" is an overclaim and is withdrawn ([r871](../reviews/REVIEW_r871.md)). *A bound assembled from the marginal, $T\ge0$ and trace-freeness holds for EVERY nonnegative symmetric hollow tensor with those marginals*, and [r872](../reviews/REVIEW_r872.md) exhibited a spectrum inside that class realising $3\sqrt{165}/10=3.853570$ at $n=12$ with a matching primal. *So no choice of dual multipliers over that data can return $\theta_0(12)$, and the two failures of the day are one failure: the primal pass and its dual read the same relaxation.* **And it DIVERGES rather than crossing over**: on the two-dimensional-block family the bound grows like $\sqrt{2n}$ ([r872](../reviews/REVIEW_r872.md)), *so it also exceeds the record's standing $3.0509$ and the two bounds are incomparable, which retires the factor-of-$2.47$ framing off the balanced product.* **The offending tensor's own $\theta$ is $\sqrt{165}/30=0.428$**, so the relaxation is loose there by nearly an order of magnitude. **THE CAUSE IS NAMED PRECISELY BY THE ROUND, AND IT IS NOT THE DUAL CHOICE**: dropping relations (B) and (C), the only inputs tying $T$ to $\lambda$, lets the relaxed $T$ dump its whole marginal budget on the best triple type ([r872](../reviews/REVIEW_r872.md)). *That reviewer exhibited a feasible primal at the offending spectrum, $T=1$ on triangles among the six within-block indices and $T=10/1711$ on cross-block triples, whose value matches the dual exactly, so the LP RELAXATION itself is optimal there and no better multipliers exist.* **Any repair must reintroduce (B) and (C).** *A further-out reading, which agrees with it: $T=c^2$ forgets the signs, the Jacobi identity is a statement about $c$, and the Killing orthogonality $\sum_{\beta\gamma}c_{\alpha\beta\gamma}c_{\delta\beta\gamma}=2(n-2)\delta_{\alpha\delta}$ is visible to $T$ only on its diagonal, where it IS the marginal.*

> **RELATION (B) COLLAPSES THE WORST SPECTRUM TO ITS TRUE VALUE, AND THE STRENGTH IS ENTIRELY IN ITS PER-INDEX ROWS.** *Working notes, Gate 2 pending, derived 2026-09-12 as the repair [r871](../reviews/REVIEW_r871.md) named.* **At the padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ spectrum at $n=12$, where the marginals-only programme returns $11/\sqrt6=4.4907$, one row of (B) gives $\theta\le\sqrt{3/2}=1.224745$, which is that object's TRUE value and below $\theta_0(12)=1.236033$.** *Three lines. The spectrum is $2c,-c,-c$ on three indices and zero on the other $63$, unit norm making $c=1/\sqrt6$, and only triples inside $\{1,2,3\}$ carry a nonzero product, so $\theta=6c^3(1+T_{123})$. Relation (B) at index $1$ reads $\sum_{\beta\gamma}\lambda_\beta T_{1\beta\gamma}=-2\lambda_1$, hollowness kills $\beta=1$, and $\lambda_2=\lambda_3=-c$ leaves $\sum_\gamma T_{12\gamma}+\sum_\gamma T_{13\gamma}=4$. Nonnegativity and total symmetry make each of those at least $T_{123}$, so $T_{123}\le2$ against the marginals' $T_{123}\le10$, and $\theta\le18c^3=\sqrt{3/2}$.* **It is TIGHT: the object itself attains it**, so no further constraint can improve this spectrum.

> **AND THE CONTRACTED FORM OF (B) IS WORSE THAN USELESS, WHICH SAYS WHERE A SUCCESSOR MUST PUT ITS MULTIPLIERS.** *Contracting (B) against $\lambda_k$ gives the single scalar $\sum_{\alpha\beta\gamma}\lambda_\alpha\lambda_\beta T_{\alpha\beta\gamma}=-2$, and the two-parameter dual it supports is* $$\lambda_\alpha\lambda_\beta\lambda_\gamma\le y+z(\lambda_\alpha\lambda_\beta+\lambda_\beta\lambda_\gamma+\lambda_\gamma\lambda_\alpha)\ \text{ at every distinct triple},\qquad \theta\le\textstyle\sum\lambda^3+(n-2)Ny-3z.$$ **Its optimum at that same spectrum is $23.48$, nineteen times the per-index answer and five times WORSE than using the marginals alone.** *So (B)'s content is carried by its $N$ rows and is destroyed by contraction, which is the same lesson as the uniform family being a strict sub-family of the dual, at a factor of nineteen instead of twenty.* **A successor puts one multiplier per index, not one per relation.**

> **AND THE PER-ROW ENGINE HAS A CLOSED GENERAL FORM: AN UPPER CONVEX HULL OVER THE PAIRS HOLLOWNESS LEAVES.** *Working notes, Gate 2 pending, derived 2026-09-12 after the round above was dispatched.* **At every index $k$,** $$R_k\ \le\ M\,h_k(\bar s_k),\qquad \bar s_k=\frac{-2\lambda_k}{M},\quad M=2(n-2),$$ *where $h_k$ is the upper convex hull of the pair cloud $\{(\tfrac12(\lambda_\beta+\lambda_\gamma),\,\lambda_\beta\lambda_\gamma)\}$ built from the indices OTHER than $k$.* **The three inputs are exactly one each**: relation (B) fixes $\bar s_k$, hollowness deletes every pair containing $k$ from the cloud, and $T\ge0$ makes the row a measure so the hull is a ceiling. *The marginal supplies only $M$, and at the padded competitor's spectrum it is slack, which is the precise sense in which a programme built on it could not reach this.*

> **THE HOLLOWNESS EXCLUSION IS THE WHOLE OF IT, AND LEAVING IT OUT LOOSENS THE ANSWER THREEFOLD.** *With pairs containing $k$ left in the cloud the hull runs from $(-c,c^2)$ to $(2c,0)$ and returns $T_{123}\le6$; with them removed it runs from $(-c,c^2)$ to $(0,0)$ and returns $T_{123}\le2$, $R_1\le4c^2$ and $\theta\le\sqrt{3/2}$, matching the three-line route in all three numbers by a construction sharing nothing with it.* **That agreement is worth recording rather than assuming**: the hull is a two-moment relaxation and so can only be weaker than a direct argument, so its meeting the direct answer says the direct argument was already extremal at that spectrum. *The general form is what a proof at every spectrum would have to evaluate, and it is finite: the cloud has at most $\binom{s}{2}+s$ distinct points at $s$ distinct eigenvalues.*

> **THE NEGATIVE ROWS NEED THE PAIR CAP RATHER THAN (B), SO THE TWO OWED INPUTS ARE COMPLEMENTARY AND NOT ALTERNATIVES.** *Working notes, Gate 2 pending, derived 2026-09-12 after the round above was dispatched.* **For $\lambda_k>0$ the per-row engine is (B) alone**: it fixes the row's $\lambda$-weighted mass at $-2\lambda_k$, nonnegativity makes the row a measure on pairs, and the largest $\lambda\lambda$-weight at that fixed $\lambda$-weight sits on the extreme pair $(\lambda_{\min},\lambda_{\min})$, giving $$R_k:=\sum_{\beta\gamma}\lambda_\beta\lambda_\gamma T_{k\beta\gamma}\ \le\ 2\lvert\lambda_{\min}\rvert\lambda_k,\qquad \lambda_k>0,$$ *whose mass demand is $2\lambda_k/\lvert\lambda_{\min}\rvert$, which at the padded competitor is $4$ against the marginal's $20$: the marginal is SLACK there, which is why a programme built on it could not see this.* **Reproduced against the three-line route by a path sharing nothing with it, returning the same $T_{123}\le2$ and the same $\sqrt{3/2}$.**

> **AND FOR $\lambda_k<0$ THE SAME ENGINE RUNS THE WRONG WAY, WHICH NAMES THE PAIR CAP'S JOB.** *Bounding $\lambda_kR_k$ above needs $R_k$ bounded BELOW there, so the extreme pair is $(\lambda_{\max},\lambda_{\min})$ with its negative product, and worse, any SUM-NEUTRAL pair with $\lambda_\beta+\lambda_\gamma=0$ carries negative product at no cost in the (B) row and can absorb the remaining mass.* **So (B) alone does not control a negative row: the row's leftover mass is what hurts, and what caps it is the PAIR CAP.** *[Lemma L31](stratum-two-negative.md)'s $\sum_\gamma T_{\alpha\beta\gamma}\le2$ bounds the mass sitting on any single $\beta$, so a row's total $2(n-2)$ must spread over at least $n-2$ values of $\beta$ and cannot pile onto one sum-neutral pair.* **That is why [r871](../reviews/REVIEW_r871.md) named BOTH (B) and the pair cap and why they are not alternatives**: (B) governs the rows at positive eigenvalues and the cap governs the rows at negative ones. *The scales are right for an $O(1)$ answer, since a leftover mass of order $n$ against eigenvalues of order $N^{-1/2}$ contributes at the same order as the rest, so the question is a constant rather than a divergence.* **What is owed is that constant, and it is the first thing the pair cap has ever been needed FOR rather than merely available.**

> **THE AUGMENTED PROGRAMME IS REFUTED AS A ROUTE, BY TWO INDEPENDENT COUNTEREXAMPLES, ONE OF THEM REALISABLE.** *Gate 2, [r877](../reviews/REVIEW_r877.md) and [r878](../reviews/REVIEW_r878.md) on identical briefs, both upholding the collapse in full and both breaking its reach.* **The mechanism, derived here as well as there: (B) and the marginal recast over unordered triples as** $$\sum_{\Delta\ni k}\sigma_\Delta\,t_\Delta=(n-4)\lambda_k,\qquad \sigma_\Delta=\lambda_a+\lambda_b+\lambda_c.$$ *So a spectrum whose objective-carrying triples have $\sigma_\Delta>0$ pays its budget OUT of the objective, and (B) then forces mass exactly where it hurts.* **The padded competitor resists only because $\sigma_{123}=0$ there**, which is a coincidence of that spectrum and not a property of the constraint. *That is the whole explanation of why the collapse looked decisive and generalises to nothing.*

> **THE REALISABLE WITNESS IS $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}\oplus W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$, AND IT CANNOT BE DISMISSED.** *At $n=12$ its spectrum is $(2d,-d,-d,2d,-d,-d,0^{60})$ with $12d^2=1$, the direct sum is Ricci-flat and satisfies first Bianchi, and the augmented programme's value there is exactly $7\sqrt3/6=2.020726$ against the object's TRUE $\sqrt3/2=0.866025$, a ratio of exactly $7/3$* ([r878](../reviews/REVIEW_r878.md), with a feasible point in exact rationals, every marginal $20$ and all three kinds of (B) row closing). **Reproduced here by the direct-sum scaling: $P$ is cubic and the blocks are orthogonal, so $\theta$ divides by $\sqrt2$ and $\theta_0$ does not.** *And the gap WIDENS: the same point gives at least $(n+2)/(4\sqrt3)$, which is $2.02$ at $n=12$ and $6.06$ at $n=40$, while $\theta_0(n)\to\sqrt2$.* **The second witness, $\tfrac1{\sqrt6}(1,1,1,-1,-1,-1,0^{60})$, gives $8/\sqrt6=3.265986$** ([r877](../reviews/REVIEW_r877.md)), *worse than the record's own standing $3.0509$, so (B) does not improve the scoreboard at all and buys a factor $11/8$ globally rather than the $11/3$ visible at one spectrum.*

> **WHAT THE ROUND LEAVES STANDING IS A CLOSED FAMILY, AND IT IS A REAL THEOREM.** *At exactly THREE nonzero eigenvalues the three (B) rows PIN $S_{12}=S_{13}=S_{23}=2$ identically rather than merely bounding them, which closes that whole family at $\theta\le\sqrt{3/2}$ in every $n$* ([r878](../reviews/REVIEW_r878.md), and [r877](../reviews/REVIEW_r877.md) independently on the pinning). **Since $\theta_0(n)>\sqrt{3/2}$ exactly for $n>6+2\sqrt7=11.2915$, that family is settled for the conjecture at every $n\ge12$ and is a genuine counterexample to it below**, which is the record's own crossover arriving from a new direction. *The collapse at the padded competitor is the $m=3$ case of it.*

> **TWO HYPOTHESES THE BLOCK MUST CARRY, AND A CORRECTION TO ITS OWN COMPARISON.** *Every claim here is scoped to TRACE-FREE spectra: the marginal and (B) together force $\sum_\alpha\lambda_\alpha=0$, so off that hyperplane the programme is infeasible and would prove everything* ([r877](../reviews/REVIEW_r877.md), [r878](../reviews/REVIEW_r878.md)). **And the $n\ge12$ hypothesis is load-bearing rather than inherited**, since the $m=3$ family's value $\sqrt{3/2}$ exceeds $\theta_0(n)$ at every $4\le n\le11$. *The "contracted (B) is worse than useless" verdict is WITHDRAWN: that comparison pitted a uniform two-parameter dual against the exact per-index LP. With the $N$ marginal multipliers retained, contracted (B) gives $5/\sqrt6=2.041241$ against the marginals-only $4.490731$, a strict improvement, and within the uniform family it improves $221/\sqrt6=90.22$ by $3.8$ times.* **So "one multiplier per index" survives at a factor $5/3$ rather than nineteen, on replaced evidence**, and the recorded $23.48$ is exactly $115/(2\sqrt6)=23.474277$. *Hollowness was also used silently twice more, killing $\gamma=k$ and $\beta=\gamma$.*

> **WHAT THIS LEAVES OWED, AND IT IS NOW A SINGLE QUESTION.** *At the balanced product the marginals-only programme already returns $\theta_0(n)$ exactly, and adding a constraint to a maximisation can only lower it while the true value is a floor, so the augmented programme returns exactly $\theta_0(n)$ there too.* **So at both extremal spectra the (B)-augmented programme is tight, which nothing in this record has managed before, and it is tight at neither of the two witnesses that refute its reach.** *That question is now ANSWERED, negatively, by the two witnesses above: the per-index (B)-augmented programme exceeds $\theta_0(12)$ at a realisable spectrum and its gap widens with $n$.* **What remains untried in [I77](../plan.md) is the PAIR CAP**, which governs the rows the (B) engine cannot reach, and any attempt on it faces the same question first: what makes a witness spectrum expensive, which the $\sigma_\Delta$ recast now answers in one line. **Two cautions the record has earned twice this week**: a relaxation tight where it was looked at has twice been useless elsewhere, and the check must cover the whole admissible range rather than one vertex.

> **A CLOSURE WAS RECORDED HERE 2026-09-21 AND IS WITHDRAWN THE SAME DAY, REFUTED BY [r1035](../reviews/REVIEW_r1035.md).** *It claimed the pair cap is dominated by [post F10](fence-posts.md) because a programme over fewer constraints has a larger feasible set.* **THE INCLUSION DOES NOT HOLD**: this programme's variables are the ten multiset classes $v_S$ with support conditions $v_S=0$ when $m_i(S)>a_i$ and a cap of $1$ on the decomposable locus, and its objective is $\sum_ia_i\mu_i^3+\tfrac12\sum_S\sigma_3(S)v_S$; F10's programme carries none of those and optimises a different functional over different variables, so neither feasible set contains the other. **And the pair cap ALREADY reaches $\sqrt{3/2}$ in the two-level case above**, below F10's $1.6059$, which refutes the closure's premise directly rather than only its inference. *The error was reading F10's constraint LIST and assuming a shared variable set; the test a domination argument owes is whether the two programmes optimise the same functional over the same variables, and it was not made.* **SO THE PAIR CAP IS UNTRIED AND THE ITEM IS LIVE.** *The withdrawn closure and its conclusion, that the item "closes on the post rather than on a run", are DELETED rather than struck through: a refuted finding left struck reads as a constraint to the next cycle, and this one had left the file asserting the closure and its refutation at once, with the closure standing unstruck two lines below the strikethrough.* *The chronology is in [ATTEMPTS.md](../ATTEMPTS.md); what [post F10](fence-posts.md) does say stands at the post itself.*
>

> **THE PAIR CAP BINDS IN THESE VARIABLES, AND A CLAIM THAT IT DOES NOT STOOD HERE FOR ONE CYCLE** ([r1053](../reviews/REVIEW_r1053.md)). *What is true is narrow: the cap summed over ALL $N(N-1)$ ordered pairs gives $\sum_jt_j\le2N(N-1)$, and $\sum_jt_j=2N(n-2)$ is the programme's FIRST EQUALITY CONSTRAINT, holding at every orthonormal basis rather than only at the vertex, so that ONE aggregation is redundant everywhere.* **The error was to conclude from it that no aggregation says anything.**

> **THE $t_j$ ADMIT THREE AGGREGATIONS, NOT ONE, BECAUSE THEY ARE DEFINED BY A PARTITION OF THE INDEX SET.** *With $A$ the positive level set, $s=\lvert A\rvert$, $p=N-s$ and $T$ totally symmetric, splitting the pairs by which part each lies in gives*
$$t_3+\tfrac13t_2\le2s(s-1),\qquad \tfrac23(t_1+t_2)\le4sp,\qquad t_0+\tfrac13t_1\le2p(p-1),$$
*whose SUM is exactly the single inequality above.* **Adding the three and keeping only the total is what threw the information away.**

> **AND THE RECORD'S OWN VERTEX VIOLATES THE FIRST, BY EXACT ARITHMETIC AND WITH NO RUN.** *The low-branch vertex at $n=12$, $s=6$ carries $t_3=120$ against an $AA$ allowance of $2s(s-1)=60$.* **The cap binds on exactly $n/2\le s\le n-2$**, since on that branch $t_3=2(n-2)s$ and the constraint reads $s\ge n-1$. *Screened by [r1053](../reviews/REVIEW_r1053.md) in exact rationals: the uncapped whole-range maximum at $n=12$ reproduces the record's printed $3.853570$, and the three constraints take it to $2.42212$, BELOW the record's standing $3.0509$.*

> **SO THE PAIR CAP IS THE ONE ADDITION IN THIS PARAGRAPH THAT WORKS**, which is what the successor paragraph two blocks below had said all along, naming it a genuine $T$-inequality the relaxation never used. *The block asserting the opposite contradicted its own neighbour for a cycle, and [r864](../reviews/REVIEW_r864.md) had already performed this part-wise summation.*

> **THIS AGREES WITH THE RECORD BY A SECOND ROUTE AND IS NOT NEW.** *[stratum-two-negative.md](stratum-two-negative.md) already gives the mean of $\lVert[v_\alpha,v_\beta]\rVert^2$ as $2(n-2)/(N-1)=4/(n+1)$ and says in its own words that against the cap $2$ the chain spends everywhere the ratio $2/(n+1)$, which is the reciprocal of the slack above.* **It was reached there from the PAIR MARGINAL and here from the TRIPLE TOTAL**, so the two are one fact, and what this block adds is the consequence for I77's programme rather than the quantity. *Found by querying the VALUE rather than the vocabulary, the search this record's own rule asks for.*

> **WHAT A SUCCESSOR MUST CLEAR, STATED SO IT CAN BE CHECKED.** *A relaxation in $T$ alone is bounded below by $3.853570$ at $n=12$ if it uses only the marginal, nonnegativity and trace-freeness, so it cannot reach the target and cannot beat the record's $3.0509$.* **Only two ways out survive, and reintroducing (B) is the FIRST of them**: add relation (B) with its own multipliers, or add the PAIR CAP, a genuine $T$-inequality this relaxation never used and one that halves on the decomposable locus, or work in $c$ rather than $T$ and consume the sign data directly. *What $4.4907$ closes is the constraint set that has the marginals alone, and it bounds the (B)-augmented programme from above rather than closing it.* *The third is where the structure is, and it is also where the record's own fence post says the marginals are $\theta$-blind, so the two statements agree: a bound must reach past the squares.*

> **THE NON-UNIFORM DUAL IS SET UP, AND A FIRST PASS AT IT PRODUCED AN INVALID BOUND, WHICH IS RECORDED SO THE NEXT ONE DOES NOT REPEAT IT.** *Working notes, Gate 2 pending, and this block states a FAILURE.* **With one multiplier per level set for each family the constraint at a triple reads** $$\lambda_\alpha\lambda_\beta\lambda_\gamma\le\tfrac13(y_i+y_j+y_k)+\tfrac16\big[z_i(\lambda_\beta+\lambda_\gamma)+z_j(\lambda_\alpha+\lambda_\gamma)+z_k(\lambda_\alpha+\lambda_\beta)\big],$$ *and the pairing gives $\theta\le\sum_ia_i\mu_i^3+(n-2)\sum_ia_iy_i-\sum_ia_i\mu_iz_i$, where the $z$ terms now CONTRIBUTE, which is the point of dropping uniformity.* **The failure**: eliminating $y_1$ and $y_2$ from the all-one and all-two constraints as though those were binding, then imposing the two mixed constraints on $z$ alone, returned $-4.282$ at the six-block spectrum of $n=12$, against a true $\theta$ of $\sqrt{165}/30=0.428$. *A negative upper bound is impossible, and the symptom names the error: the objective increases in each $y_i$, so each must be set to the MAXIMUM of the lower bounds every constraint containing its level set imposes, and the four constraints couple both $y$'s.* **So the repair is a genuine coupled four-variable programme rather than a substitution**, and no number from it is recorded here. **A SECOND PASS, done as that coupled programme, came out UNBOUNDED BELOW**, which is also impossible: the primal is feasible, the actual tensor being a feasible point, so duality forbids an unbounded dual. *The hazard both passes share is named here rather than worked around: the $\tfrac13$ and $\tfrac16$ factors were derived for the CLASS-SUM formulation, where $v_S$ totals $T$ over the ordered triples of a class, and carrying them into a per-index dual double-counts.* **So what is owed is the dual written from the ordered-triple primal directly, with its coefficients counted rather than inherited**, and until that is done this line records two failures and no bound. *Two arithmetic impossibilities in one attempt is the signal to stop and rebuild the formulation, not to try a third variation of it.*

#### From [I75](../plan.md): the $\Lambda^4$ obstructions satisfy two free relations, so two eigenvalues kill them outright

**Derived 2026-09-12. GATE 2 PASSED, [r995](../reviews/REVIEW_r995.md) and [r996](../reviews/REVIEW_r996.md) on identical briefs**, *agreeing on all ten rows independently: nine CONFIRMED, the sharpness claim PLAUSIBLE, nothing refuted, with three statement corrections carried below.* *Write $\mathfrak b(P_\mu)\in\Lambda^4$ for the obstruction at each distinct eigenvalue, so that $\mathrm{Alt}(W^k)=\tfrac16\sum_\mu\mu^k\,\mathfrak b(P_\mu)$ and [I75](../plan.md)'s requirement is that every one of them vanishes.* **Two linear relations hold for free**: $$\sum_\mu\mathfrak b(P_\mu)=\mathfrak b(\mathrm{Id})=0,\qquad \sum_\mu\mu\,\mathfrak b(P_\mu)=6\,\mathrm{Alt}(W)=0,$$ *the first because $\mathfrak b(\mathrm{Id})=0$, which is a THEOREM at every $n$ rather than the screen this block first recorded: $\mathrm{comp}(\mathrm{Id})_{ijkl}=\delta_{ik}\delta_{jl}-\delta_{il}\delta_{jk}$ is the Kulkarni-Nomizu square of the metric, and its cyclic sum cancels in pairs by the symmetry of $\delta$ ALONE, with no hypothesis and no dimension restriction; and the second because it IS first Bianchi.* **AND THE FIRST IS NOW TYPED**, [altCoeff_id_eq_zero](../lean/HamiltonsCubic/Base/AltPart.lean), standard axioms, *which is what both readers asked for: the record carried it as a screen and the kernel now carries it as a lemma.* **The proof is branchless**: the six products cancel in pairs differing only by the order of a conjunction and the orientation of an equation, so permutative normalisation aligns them as atoms and `ring` closes it, *where splitting on the six conditions instead exhausts the recursion limit across its sixty-four branches.* **So the obstructions lie in the kernel of a $2\times s$ Vandermonde at $s$ distinct eigenvalues**, and the count decides the item.

> **AT TWO DISTINCT EIGENVALUES THEY VANISH, SO [I73](../plan.md) IMPLIES [I75](../plan.md).** *A $2\times2$ Vandermonde on distinct nodes is invertible, so $\mathfrak b(P_{\mu_1})=\mathfrak b(P_{\mu_2})=0$ and every $\mathrm{Alt}(W^k)=0$ with no further hypothesis.* **That supplies the route's unnamed FIRST link on the whole two-eigenvalue locus**, which is exactly where [L105](bounds-relaxations.md) already finishes, so the destination [I73](../plan.md) now carries [I75](../plan.md) with it. *Screened: a two-eigenvalue Ricci-flat diagonal tensor at $n=4$ with weights $2,2,-1,-1,-1,-1$ returns $\mathrm{Alt}(W^2)=0$ exactly, and the $n=5$ pentagon has $W^2=\mathrm{Id}$, so $\mathrm{Alt}(W^2)=\mathrm{Alt}(\mathrm{Id})=0$ by hand.*

> **AT THREE IT IS ONE CONDITION, AND THE ARGUMENT STOPS EXACTLY WHERE THE COUNTEREXAMPLE STARTS.** *The kernel is then a line, spanned by $(\mu_2-\mu_3,\mu_3-\mu_1,\mu_1-\mu_2)$, so $\mathfrak b(P_\mu)=c\,v_\mu$ and $\mathrm{Alt}(W^2)=\tfrac16c\,(\mu_1-\mu_2)(\mu_2-\mu_3)(\mu_1-\mu_3)$ up to sign.* **So at three distinct eigenvalues the whole $\Lambda^4$ family reduces to the single condition $\mathrm{Alt}(W^2)=0$.** *And $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ has spectrum $\{2c,-c,0\}$, precisely $s=3$: the first case this argument does not cover is the object where the link is known to fail, so the reduction is sharp at the obstruction rather than short of it.* **A consequence worth its own line**: $\mathrm{Alt}(W^3)=\big(\sum_\mu\mu\big)\mathrm{Alt}(W^2)$ at $s=3$, the sum being over the DISTINCT values, *screened at $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ at three scalings with ratios $1$, $2$ and $\tfrac12$ against sums $1$, $2$ and $\tfrac12$.*

> **AND IN GENERAL THE ITEM IS A FINITE LIST.** *Requiring $\mathrm{Alt}(W^k)=0$ for the CONSECUTIVE block $k=2,\dots,s-1$ adds $\max(s-2,0)$ rows, which with the two free ones completes a square Vandermonde and forces every $\mathfrak b(P_\mu)=0$.* **The block must be consecutive, and that is a correction rather than a detail**: on real nodes a non-consecutive choice can be singular, witnessed at spectrum $\{-1,0,1\}$ with $k=0,1,3$, where the matrix has rank $2$ and $\mathrm{Alt}(W^3)=0$ therefore does NOT imply $\mathrm{Alt}(W^2)=0$ ([r995](../reviews/REVIEW_r995.md), reproduced here). *And the obstruction tuple lives in $\ker V\otimes\Lambda^4$, of dimension $(s-2)\dim\Lambda^4$, so the count is of CONDITIONS and each is $\binom n4$ scalar equations.* **So [I75](../plan.md) is exactly $s-2$ conditions at $s$ distinct eigenvalues, not an infinite family**, and the $\Lambda^4$ test's "for every $k\ge1$" is spent by $k\le s-1$. *What it does not give is any bound on $s$ at a maximiser, which is where the item still needs [I73](../plan.md) or a spectral count of its own.*

> **AND $\sqrt{3/2}$ IS NOT THE THRESHOLD THAT BOUNDS $s$** ([W7](INDEX-traps.md), Gate 2 by [r1050](../reviews/REVIEW_r1050.md) and [r1051](../reviews/REVIEW_r1051.md)). *$S^5\times S^7$ at $n=12$ is a critical point at $\theta=1.2282390$, ABOVE $\sqrt{3/2}=1.2247449$, carrying THREE distinct eigenvalues ([ungraded-slack.md](ungraded-slack.md)).* **So "critical AND above $\sqrt{3/2}$" does not give $s\le2$.**

> **A HIGHER THRESHOLD IS NOT TOUCHED BY IT, AND SAYING OTHERWISE KILLED THE ONE ROUTE THE AUDIT LEFT OPEN.** *The witness sits BELOW $\theta_0(12)=1.2360331$, so "critical and above $\theta_0(n)$" survives this counterexample entirely and is the live candidate for [I73](../plan.md).* **An earlier version of this block wrote that I73 "needs something strictly stronger than criticality at a value", which declares that candidate dead on evidence that does not reach it.** *The claim has now been wrong in both directions on the same witness: first that the threshold clause was load-bearing, then that no threshold helps. What the witness settles is one value of one threshold.*

> **AND THE $\theta_0$ CANDIDATE FALLS AT $n=6$, ONCE** ([r1052](../reviews/REVIEW_r1052.md)). *Jensen's second Einstein metric on $S^3\times S^3$ is critical at $\theta=\sqrt{6/5}=1.095445115$, STRICTLY ABOVE $\theta_0(6)=1.054092553$, with three eigenvalues $\tfrac5{24},0,-\tfrac18$ at multiplicities $3,7,5$.* **$\mathbb{CP}^3$ does NOT make it twice**: that point sits AT $\theta_0(6)$ exactly, so it refutes the candidate only in its $\ge$ form, and the record states the candidate as "above" everywhere else. *A first version of this block counted both and said "TWICE", which needs a threshold the paragraph had silently loosened.*

> **AND NOTHING HERE IS NARROWED TO $n\ge12$, WHICH A FIRST VERSION OF THIS BLOCK CLAIMED.** *The record DOES carry the propagation mechanism, [L164](stratum-two-negative.md)'s padding preserving norm, $\theta$ and criticality; it fails only because $\theta_0$ strictly increases with $n$ while padding freezes $\theta$, and below the crossover $6+2\sqrt7=11.2915$ nothing makes $\theta_0(n)$ a ceiling at all.* **THE ONE CANDIDATE COUNTEREXAMPLE THE RECORD NAMED AT $n\ge12$ IS GONE, AND THE OBSTRUCTION CLASS IS NOT**: [post F43](bounds-relaxations.md)'s $n=12$, kernel-free, THREE-eigenvalue spectrum $(30,20,16)$ passes every per-row condition at $\theta=1.2917>\theta_0(12)=1.2360331$, and [I24a](../plan.md) put it under the full pinned system, the three relations of [DERIVE-t11](../reviews/DERIVE-t11.md) with $T\ge0$ over all $\binom{66}3=45{,}760$ triples, where it returns $\max\theta=0.9242643$. **It is therefore NOT realisable in the pinned class**, and the gap is a factor rather than a margin. *The control passed and could have failed, the balanced $S^5\times S^7$ product returning $\theta_0(12)$ to seven digits over the same system.* **ONE WITNESS, NOT THE CLASS.** *[r1057](../reviews/REVIEW_r1057.md) screened the same multiplicities with the third eigenvalue moved and got LP maxima of $1.3193$, $1.3102$ and $1.2904$, all ABOVE $\theta_0(12)$, so nearby spectra survive the relaxation and the obstruction class is open.* **AND $1.2917$ IS NOT THE PER-ROW VALUE**, [DERIVE-t11](../reviews/DERIVE-t11.md) saying in its own words that the budget *recovers part of that pinning ($1.2917$ against the per-row $1.4078$)*, so the budget is already a CROSS-ROW relation. *The per-row maximum at this spectrum is $1.4078288$, and what the full system adds over the per-row family is $1.4078288$ against $0.9242643$, a factor of $1.5232$.* **What this does NOT establish is the restricted candidate**: no counterexample at $n\ge12$ is a different statement from a proof, and the record still has no route to one.

> **Further down, $s$ reaches $10$ and $15$ at off-catalogue generic Newton points of $\mathcal W_6$**, at $\theta=0.054718$ and $0.091388$, SCREENED at residual below $10^{-9}$ and cited by [bounds.md](bounds.md) AGAINST the catalogue rather than as part of it; [r336](../reviews/REVIEW_r336.md) carries twenty such points spanning $s=10$ to $15$ at residual $\le5\times10^{-15}$, while the $\theta=0.091388$ point is single-sourced.

> **BUT A LARGE $s$ IS NO EVIDENCE AGAINST THIS ITEM, AND A FIRST VERSION OF THIS BLOCK SAID IT WAS.** *At every coordinate-diagonal $W$, $\mathrm{comp}(W)_{ijkl}$ is supported on $\{i,j\}=\{k,l\}$, so each cyclic term of $\mathrm{Alt}$ needs a pair coincidence that four distinct indices forbid, and $\mathrm{Alt}$ vanishes on repeated indices by antisymmetry.* **So $\mathrm{Alt}(W^k)=0$ there at every $k$ and every $s$, with no criticality used**, and a point with $s=15$ can satisfy all $13$ of its rows. *The row count measures the SIZE of the obligation and never its failure.*

> **WHAT DOES REFUTE THE CONCLUSION OVER THE CRITICAL SET IS ALREADY IN THE PLAN'S OWN I75 ROW**: padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$, critical at $\theta=\sqrt{3/2}$ EXACTLY with $\mathrm{Alt}(W^3)=c^3\star\ne0$. *It is sharper than anything above, sitting at the boundary rather than far below it, and it was on the record throughout.*

> **A SECOND PROOF SETTLES ROWS 6, 7 AND 8 AT ONCE, AND IT IS CLEANER THAN THE VANDERMONDE** ([r996](../reviews/REVIEW_r996.md)). *The minimal polynomial gives $W^3=e_1W^2-e_2W+e_3\mathrm{Id}$, and the two free relations kill the last two terms, so $\mathrm{Alt}(W^3)=e_1\mathrm{Alt}(W^2)$ with $e_1$ the first symmetric function of the MINIMAL polynomial's roots.* **That is the distinct sum by construction**, which is why the multiplicity reading was never available: $\sum_\mu m_\mu\mu=\operatorname{tr}_{\Lambda^2}W=0$ at every Weyl tensor, so it would force $\mathrm{Alt}(W^3)=0$, and $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ has $\mathrm{Alt}(W^3)=c^3\star\ne0$.

> **AND AT $n=4$ THE ITEM HAS A NAME** ([r996](../reviews/REVIEW_r996.md)). *There $\mathfrak b(R)=(\operatorname{tr}R_+-\operatorname{tr}R_-)\star$, so [I75](../plan.md) reads exactly as $W_+$ and $W_-$ being ISOSPECTRAL.* **That explains the $6$ as $\lVert\star\rVert^2$** and makes every $\mathfrak b(P_\mu)$ an integer multiple of $\star$, which is what puts cheap tensors on both sides of the condition.

### $P$ on the diagonal locus, proved

#### Lemma L231, $P$ on the diagonal locus IS the weighted-graph functional

**At every $n$ and every weight $w$ of $\mathcal D_n$, $P(\mathrm{diag}(w))=\tfrac12\big(\sum_{a,b}w_{ab}^3+\operatorname{tr}w^3\big)$.** *Machine-checked, `diagCubicFormula` in [SharpDiag.lean](../lean/HamiltonsCubic/Base/SharpDiag.lean), standard three axioms and no `sorry`.* **Where the identity at the head of this file stood on four test points, it is now a theorem.**

**THE EVALUATION IS `sharpQF_diagOp`**: $\langle(\mathrm{diag}\,w)^\#\varphi_{ab},\varphi_{ab}\rangle=\sum_{d\notin\{a,b\}}w_{ad}w_{bd}$ at every SYMMETRIC $w$, with no hollowness and no row-sum hypothesis. *The mechanism is that $\mathrm{ad}_{\varphi_{ab}}\circ\mathrm{diag}\,w$ SQUARED is diagonal on the $\varphi$ family, eigenvalue $-w_{ad}w_{bd}$ on $\varphi_{ad}$ and on $\varphi_{bd}$ and zero elsewhere, so the ordered-pair sum is four copies of one number per index $d$.* **Symmetry is necessary there and an exact hollow witness shows it**: at $n=3$ with $w_{02}=w_{12}=1$ and $w_{20}=w_{21}=0$, the pairing is $\tfrac12$ against the closed form's $1$, because an asymmetric weight sends a bivector partly into the SYMMETRIC matrices where $\varphi_{ab}$ is no longer an eigenvector.

**THE ASSEMBLY SPLITS INTO THE TWO TERMS, AND THAT SPLIT IS WHAT DISCHARGES [L57](problem.md).** $\operatorname{tr}W^3=\tfrac12\langle M^{\circ2},M\rangle$ is `trCube_diagOp`, which needs HOLLOWNESS ALONE and holds at every $w$ symmetric or not, and $\langle W,W^\#\rangle=\tfrac12\langle M^2,M\rangle$ is `sharpPair_diagOp`. *`sharpPair` reaches `sharpQF` through `sharpOp` and `sharpBil`, so the polarisation is collapsed back to the quadratic form first, leaving the two pairs $(i,j)$ and $(j,i)$, and $\varphi_{ji}=-\varphi_{ij}$ makes the second ADD rather than cancel.*

**THE RECORD SAID L57'S BRIDGE AND THIS LEMMA WERE THE SAME STATEMENT AND THEY ARE NOT** ([r1001](../reviews/REVIEW_r1001.md)). *The bridge is the CONJUNCTION of those two identities where this lemma is their SUM, so it follows from the bridge and does not imply it, and a cancelling pair of errors satisfies the sum and neither conjunct, which is the pair [post F11](second-order.md) says must be priced together.* **What discharges L57 is the two conjuncts being proved SEPARATELY**, which they are.

**Hollowness enters twice and symmetry three times, and $\mathcal D_n$'s ROW-SUM clause is never used.** *So the identity holds on a strictly wider class than the locus, which the statement above does not claim and a later cycle may want.*

*Gate 2: [r1001](../reviews/REVIEW_r1001.md), one reviewer, the round comparing a Lean layer against the record rather than checking an argument. CONFIRMED on the definitions, the normalising constants, and non-vacuity; it REFUTED the "same statement" wording and found `trCube_diagOp`'s symmetry binder superfluous, both carried.*

#### Lemma L232, $\theta=\theta_{p,q}$ at every sphere product

**At every split with $p,q\ge2$ and every $t>0$ with $\lVert\mathrm{diag}(t\,w_{p,q})\rVert=1$, $\theta=\theta_{p,q}=\sqrt{2(n-1)(p-1)(q-1)/((n-2)pq)}$.** *Machine-checked, `sphereProductValue` and `theta_sphereProduct_value` in [SphereValue.lean](../lean/HamiltonsCubic/Base/SphereValue.lean), standard three axioms and no `sorry`.* **This is the lower half of $\sup\ge\Theta_n$**, and what remains for that is choosing the split, arithmetic on $\theta_{p,q}$ and on no curvature operator.

**THE WHOLE EVALUATION COLLAPSES TO ONE IDENTITY**: $\mathrm{diagP}(w)=(n-1)\lVert\mathrm{diag}(w)\rVert^2$ at the sphere weight. *Then $P$ at the unit-norm member is $t(n-1)$, and the square root is never expanded, since $(t(n-1))^2$ and $\theta_{p,q}^2$ are the same rational function of $p$ and $q$.* **What holds it up is the pair of row sums $(p-1)\alpha=q$ and $(q-1)\beta=p$ read blockwise, and it is NOT a general fact about weights.**

*$\operatorname{tr}w^3$ is read as $\sum_{a,b}(w^2)_{ab}w_{ba}$ rather than as a triple sum, which needs only that $w^2$ is again block-constant instead of counting ordered triples of distinct indices per block.* **The reusable piece is `block_pair_sum`**, an ordered-pair sum whose summand depends only on the block pattern, carrying the entrywise square, the entrywise cube and the trace of the cube alike.

*Checked independently at fifteen splits of $n=4$ to $8$ by [screen_I66_sphere_product_anchor.py](../scripts/misc/screen_I66_sphere_product_anchor.py), the balanced $n=6$ row returning $\sqrt{10}/3$, which no part of the proof uses.*
